Args>\n    void construct(U* const p, Args&&... args) const {\n        if constexpr (sizeof...(Args) > 0) {\n            ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n        }\n    }\n    template <typename U>\n    void destroy(U* const) const noexcept {}\n\n    template <typename U>\n    bool operator==(const NoInitAllocator<U>&) const noexcept { return true; }\n    template normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\ndouble blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n    \n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n    \n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n    \n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n    \n    return price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\nusing OptionBuffer = std::vector<OptionInput, NoInitAllocator<OptionInput>>;\nusing PriceBuffer = std::vector<double, NoInitAllocator<double>>;\n\n// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set\nvoid generateOptions(OptionBuffer& options, const size_t numOptions) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    options.resize(numOptions);\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly\n        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];\n        options[i] = base;\n        \n        // Add some variation for larger datasets\n        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));\n        options[i].spot *= factor;\n        options[i].strike *= factor;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const OptionBuffer& options,\n                     const PriceBuffer& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n    \n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n        \n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\", \n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n        \n        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);\n            allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation against known values\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t numOptions = 10000;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of options: %zu\\n\", numOptions);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? Args>\n    void construct(U* const p, Args&&... args) const {\n        if constexpr (sizeof...(Args) > 0) {\n            ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n        }\n    }\n    template <typename U>\n    void destroy(U* const) const noexcept {}\n\n    template <typename U>\n    bool operator==(const NoInitAllocator<U>&) const noexcept { return true; }\n    template <typename U>\n    bool operator!=(const NoInitAllocator<U>&) const noexcept { return false; }\n};\n\nenum OptionType {\n    CALL = 0,\n    PUT = 1\n};\n\nstruct OptionInput {\n    int type;           // CALL or PUT\n    double strike;      // Strike price\n    double spot;        // Spot price\n    double q;           // Dividend yield\n    double r;           // Risk-free rate\n    double t;           // Time to maturity\n    double vol;         // Volatility\n    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\ninline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\ninline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\ndouble blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n    \n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - K * discount * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n    \n    return price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\nusing OptionBuffer = std::vector<OptionInput, NoInitAllocator<OptionInput>>;\nusing PriceBuffer = std::vector<double, NoInitAllocator<double>>;\n\n// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set\n// Smallest number of options that justifies handing a chunk to another thread;\n// below that the barrier/fork cost of an extra thread outweighs the work.\nconstexpr size_t kMinOptionsPerThread = 1024;\n\n// Team size used for every loop over the option arrays. Keeping it identical\n// everywhere makes the static schedules line up, so each thread reads and\n// writes the pages it first touched.\nint pricingThreads(const size_t numOptions) noexcept {\n    const size_t byWork = numOptions / kMinOptionsPerThread;\n    const size_t maxThreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());\n    return static_cast<int>(std::max<size_t>(1, std::min(maxThreads, byWork)));\n}\n\nvoid generateOptions(OptionBuffer& options, const size_t numOptions) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    options.resize(numOptions);\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(pricingThreads(numOptions))\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n        \n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\", \n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n        \n        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);\n            allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation against known values\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t numOptions = 10000;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; This keeps the large\n// input/output buffers out of the master thread's NUMA node: the pages are\n// first touched by the OpenMP loops themselves, which use a matching static\n// schedule so that each thread owns the pages it works on.\ntemplate <typename T>\nstruct NoInitAllocator {\n    using value_type = T;\n\n    NoInitAllocator() noexcept = default;\n    template <typename U>\n    NoInitAllocator(const NoInitAllocator<U>&) noexcept {}\n\n    T* allocate(const std::size_t n) {\n        return static_cast<T*>(::operator new(n * sizeof(T)));\n    }\n    void deallocate(T* const p, const std::size_t) noexcept {\n        ::operator delete(static_cast<void*>(p));\n    }\n\n    // Value-initialization (vector::resize with no argument) becomes a no-op;\n    // any explicit initializer is still honoured.\n    template <typename U, typename... Args>\n    void construct(U* const p, Args&&... args) const {\n        if constexpr (sizeof...(Args) > 0) {\n            ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n        }\n    }\n    template <typename U>\n    void destroy(U* const) const noexcept {}\n\n    template <typename U>\n    bool operator==(const NoInitAllocator<U>&) const noexcept { return true; }\n    template <typename U>\n    bool operator!=(const NoInitAllocator<U>&) const noexcept { return false; }\n};\n\nenum OptionType {\n    CALL = 0,\n    PUT = 1\n};\n\nstruct OptionInput {\n    int type;           // CALL or PUT\n    double strike;      // Strike price\n    double spot;        // Spot price\n    double q;           // Dividend yield\n    double r;           // Risk-free rate\n    double t;           // Time to maturity\n    double vol;         // Volatility\n    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\ninline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\ninline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\ndouble blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n    \n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n    \n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n    \n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n    \n    return price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\nusing OptionBuffer = std::vector<OptionInput, NoInitAllocator<OptionInput>>;\nusing PriceBuffer = std::vector<double, NoInitAllocator<double>>;\n\n// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set\n// Smallest number of options that justifies handing a chunk to another thread;\n// below that the barrier/fork cost of an extra thread outweighs the work.\nconstexpr size_t kMinOptionsPerThread = 1024;\n\n// Team size used for every loop over the option arrays. Keeping it identical\n// everywhere makes the static schedules line up, so each thread reads and\n// writes the pages it first touched.\nint pricingThreads(const size_t numOptions) noexcept {\n    const size_t byWork = numOptions / kMinOptionsPerThread;\n    const size_t maxThreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());\n    return static_cast<int>(std::max<size_t>(1, std::min(maxThreads, byWork)));\n}\n\nvoid generateOptions(OptionBuffer& options, const size_t numOptions) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    options.resize(numOptions);\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(pricingThreads(numOptions))\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly\n        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];\n        options[i] = base;\n        \n        // Add some variation for larger datasets\n        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));\n        options[i].spot *= factor;\n        options[i].strike *= factor;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const OptionBuffer& options,\n                     const PriceBuffer& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n    \n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n        \n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\", \n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n        \n        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);\n            allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation against known values\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t numOptions = 10000;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; This keeps the large\n// input/output buffers out of the master thread's NUMA node: the pages are\n// first touched by the OpenMP loops themselves, which use a matching static\n// schedule so that each thread owns the pages it works on.\ntemplate <typename T>\nstruct NoInitAllocator {\n    using value_type = T;\n\n    NoInitAllocator() noexcept = default;\n    template <typename U>\n    NoInitAllocator(const NoInitAllocator<U>&) noexcept {}\n\n    T* allocate(const std::size_t n) {\n        return static_cast<T*>(::operator new(n * sizeof(T)));\n    }\n    void deallocate(T* const p, const std::size_t) noexcept {\n        ::operator delete(static_cast<void*>(p));\n    }\n\n    // Value-initialization (vector::resize with no argument) becomes a no-op;\n    // any explicit initializer is still honoured.\n    template <typename U, typename... The pricing loop is now OpenMP-parallel and results are bit-identical to the original.\n\n## Changes\n\n**`black-scholes/black_scholes.cpp`**\n- **Pricing loop** (`main:~253`): `#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)`. `blackScholes` is pure and each iteration writes one distinct element, so no synchronization is needed and the arithmetic per option is untouched — output is bit-exact, not just close.\n- **NUMA-aware allocation**: added `NoInitAllocator`, an allocator whose `construct()` is a no-op for value-initialization, so `vector::resize()` no longer touches every page from the master thread (which would pin ~400 MB to one NUMA node on this 2-socket box). Pages are instead first-touched by the parallel loops.\n- **Parallel first-touch / warm-up**: `generateOptions` is parallelized, and the result buffer is zero-filled in a parallel loop before the timer starts — equivalent to the original serial zero-init on construction, but it places the pages per-thread and pays the page faults and thread-pool startup outside the measured region.\n- **Consistent team size**: `pricingThreads()` is used by all three loops so their `static` schedules align and each thread touches the memory it later reads/writes. It also caps the team at one thread per 1024 options, which avoids a slowdown at small `-n` (the default `-n 10000` went from 0.96 ms serial to 0.25 ms; with a full 256-thread team it was *slower* than serial, ~10 ms, because a 256-thread fork/barrier costs more than the work).\n- Prints the thread count; `-r` copies into a plain `std::vector<double>` for `print_results` (untimed) so `common/` stayed untouched.\n\n**`black-scholes/CMakeLists.txt`**: `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)` + link `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`.
